Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the molecules framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 127. The resulting operator achieves bound O(382/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 127 + O(1/n^382). Summing over all modes and applying the molecules convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
